T_STOP)\n   314→        return time[inside], x[inside], y[inside], likelihood[inside]\n   315→\n   316→    # smooth the x, y postion, and compute the speed, filter by the likelihood threshold > 0.90\n   317→    def _velocity(self, track):\n   318→        \"\"\"Combined velocity over the valid frames, NaN elsewhere.\"\"\"\n   319→        time, x, y, likelihood = track\n   320→        speed = np.full(time.size, np.nan)\n   321→        if time.size < 2:                              # a few trials have no frame times\n   322→            return time, speed\n   323→\n   324→        sigma = SMOOTH_SIGMA_MS / 1000 / np.median(np.diff(time))\n   325→        valid = likelihood > LIKELIHOOD_CUT            # x and y are NaN below this\n   326→\n   327→        # smooth and differentiate inside each run of tracked frames, never across a gap\n   328→        for start, stop in _runs(valid):\n   329→            if stop - start < 2:                       # a lone frame has no velocity\n   330→                continue\n   331→            sx = gaussian_filter1d(x[start:stop], sigma, mode='mirror')\n   332→            sy = gaussian_filter1d(y[start:stop], sigma, mode='mirror')\n   333→            speed[start:stop] = np.hypot(np.gradient(sx, time[start:stop]),\n   334→                                         np.gradient(sy, time[start:stop]))\n   335→        return time, speed\n   336→\n   337→    def _view_scale(self, velocity):\n   338→        \"\"\"The percentile that puts one camera's velocity on a comparable scale.\"\"\"\n   339→        pooled = np.concatenate([velocity[trial][1] for trial in self.trials])\n   340→        return np.nanpercentile(pooled, NORMALIZE_PCT)\n   341→\n   342→    # the tongue is seen by both cameras, on different pixel scales, so each view is\n   343→    # binned and divided by its own percentile before the two are averaged\n   344→    def tongue_velocity(self):\n   345→        \"\"\"Binned tongue velocity, as (n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n   346→        side = np.array([_bin_frames(*self.side_tongue[trial]) for trial in self.trials])\n   347→        bottom = np.array([_bin_frames(*self.bottom_tongue[trial]) for trial in self.trials])\n   348→        side = side / self.side_scale\n   349→        bottom = bottom / self.bottom_scale\n   350→        return _mean_over_available(side, bottom)      # one view alone where the other is missing\n   351→\n   352→    # only one paw stays tracked through the delay, so it is used on its own, in pixels\n   353→    def paw_velocity(self):\n   354→        \"\"\"Binned paw velocity, as (n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n   355→        return np.array([_bin_frames(*self.paw[trial]) for trial in self.trials])\n   356→\n   357→    # motion energy is already one number per frame, so it only needs binning\n   358→    def motion_energy(self):\n   359→        \"\"\"Binned motion energy, as (n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n   360→        rows = []\n   361→        for trial in self.trials:\n   362→            time = self._frame_time(trial, SIDE)       # motion energy follows the side camera\n   363→            inside = (time >= T_START) & (time <= T_STOP)\n   364→            rows.append(_bin_frames(time[inside], self.energy[trial][inside]))\n   365→        return np.array(rows)\n   366→\n   367→    def outputs(self):\n   368→        \"\"\"The three streams, each cut in two at its own session median.\"\"\"\n   369→        tongue = self.tongue_velocity()\n   370→        paw = self.paw_velocity()\n   371→        energy = self.motion_energy()\n   372→        return (_discretize(tongue, np.nanpercentile(tongue, SPLIT_PCT)),\n   373→                _discretize(paw, np.nanpercentile(paw, SPLIT_PCT)),\n   374→                _discretize(energy, np.nanpercentile(energy, SPLIT_PCT)))\n   375→\n   376→\n   377→# ------------------------------------------------------------------- neural --\n   378→# Spike times are already on the behavior clock and relative to trial start, so aligning\n   379→# to the go cue is one subtraction. Quality is a free text label written with either case\n   380→# and with a few typos, so it is matched in lower case.\n   381→\n   382→def _quality(cluster):\n   383→    \"\"\"Quality label of a cluster; a handful carry no label at all.\"\"\"\n   384→    label = cluster['quality']\n   385→    return label.strip().lower() if isinstance(label, str) else ''\n   386→\n   387→\n   388→class Neural:\n   389→    \"\"\"The QC-passing units of a session, as smoothed firing rates.\"\"\"\n   390→\n   391→    def __init__(self, obj, probes, trials):\n   392→        self.obj = obj\n   393→        self.go_cue = trial_column(obj['bp'], 'ev', 'goCue')\n   394→        self.clusters = [cluster for probe in probes                # probes concatenated\n   395→                         for cluster in self._probe_clusters(probe)\n   396→                         if _quality(cluster) not in QUALITY_DROP]\n   397→\n   398→        # two sessions keep running after the recording stops, leaving trials with no spikes\n   399→        last = max(int(np.asarray(cluster['trial'], int).max(initial=0))\n   400→                   for cluster in self.clusters)\n   401→        self.trials = np.asarray([t for t in trials if t < last])   # trial numbers are 1 based\n   402→\n   403→    def _probe_clusters(self, probe):\n   404→        \"\"\"One dict per cluster on the given probe, however the file stores them.\"\"\"\n   405→        entry = self.obj['clu'][probe - 1]\n   406→        if isinstance(entry, dict) and isinstance(entry['tm'], list):\n   407→            # v7.3: one entry per probe, each field holding one array per cluster\n   408→            return [{field: entry[field][i] for field in entry}\n   409→                    for i in range(len(entry['tm']))]\n   410→        return self.obj['clu']                     # v5: one entry per cluster, single probe\n   411→\n   412→    def spike_count(self, cluster):\n   413→        \"\"\"Spikes of one cluster per trial and bin, aligned to the go cue.\"\"\"\n   414→        spike_trial = np.asarray(cluster['trial'], int) - 1         # trial numbers are 1 based\n   415→        spike_time = np.asarray(cluster['trialtm'], float) - self.go_cue[spike_trial]\n   416→\n   417→        # count spikes into a trial by bin grid; those outside the window fall off the edges\n   418→        trial_edges = np.arange(self.go_cue.size + 1) - 0.5\n   419→        counts, _, _ = np.histogram2d(spike_trial, spike_time, bins=[trial_edges, BIN_EDGES])\n   420→        return counts[self.trials]                                  # only the trials we keep\n   421→\n   422→    def rates(self):\n   423→        \"\"\"Firing rate of every unit above MIN_RATE, as (n_units, n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n   424→        sigma = RATE_SD_MS / 1000 / BIN\n   425→        rates = []\n   426→        for cluster in self.clusters:\n   427→            rate = self.spike_count(cluster) / BIN                 # counts -> Hz\n   428→            if rate.mean() > MIN_RATE:\n   429→                rates.append(gaussian_filter1d(rate, sigma, axis=1, mode='reflect'))\n   430→        return np.asarray(rates, np.float32)\n   431→\n   432→\n   433→# ------------------------------------------------------------------ session --\n   434→\n   435→def process_session(name, datadir):\n   436→    \"\"\"Convert one session into per-trial neural, input, and output arrays.\"\"\"\n   437→    obj = load_mat(session_file(name, 'data_structure', datadir))\n   438→    info = trial_info(obj['bp'])\n   439→\n   440→    neural = Neural(obj, SESSIONS[name], info.index)\n   441→    info = info.loc[neural.trials]                 # the three per-trial values are repeated across bins so all six outputs share one array\n   448→    out = np.empty((n_trials, len(OUTPUT_NAMES), N_BINS), np.int8)\n   449→    out[:, 0, :] = info['lick_direction'].to_numpy()[:, None]\n   450→    out[:, 1, :] = info['context'].to_numpy()[:, None]\n   451→    out[:, 2, :] = info['outcome'].to_numpy()[:, None]\n   452→    out[:, 3, :] = tongue\n   453→    out[:, 4, :] = paw\n   454→    out[:, 5, :] = energy\n   455→\n   456→    inp = np.tile(TIME.astype(np.float32), (n_trials, 1, 1))       # (n_trials, 1, N_BINS)\n   457→\n   458→    return {\n   459→        'session_id': name,\n   460→        'subject': name.split('_')[0],\n   461→        'neural': [np.ascontiguousarray(rates[:, t, :]) for t in range(n_trials)],\n   462→        'input': [inp[t] for t in range(n_trials)],\n   463→        'output': [out[t] for t in range(n_trials)],\n   464→        'n_trials': n_trials,\n   465→        'n_units': int(rates.shape[0]),\n   466→    }\n   467→\n   468→\n   469→def assemble(results):\n   470→    \"\"\"Stitch per-session results into the target dictionary.\"\"\"\n   471→    subjects = sorted({r['subject'] for r in results})\n   472→    sub_ix = {s: i for i, s in enumerate(subjects)}\n   473→\n   474→    return {\n   475→        'neural': [r['neural'] for r in results],\n   476→        'input': [r['input'] for r in results],\n   477→        'output': [r['output'] for r in results],\n   478→\n   479→        'subjects': subjects,\n   480→        'subject_idx': np.array([sub_ix[r['subject']] for r in results], np.int32),\n   481→\n   482→        'brain_regions': [BRAIN_REGION],\n   483→        'brain_region_idx': [np.zeros(r['n_units'], np.int32) for r in results],\n   484→\n   485→        'input_names': INPUT_NAMES,\n   486→        'output_names': OUTPUT_NAMES,\n   487→        'output_values': OUTPUT_VALUES,\n   488→\n   489→        'metadata': {\n   490→            'task_description':\n   491→                'Head-fixed mice lick left or right for water. In the delayed-response '\n   492→                'context a tone instructs the direction, a delay follows, and an auditory '\n   493→                'go cue opens the response window; in the water-cued context all cues are '\n   494→                'omitted and water arrives at a random port. t0\n   552→        print('[%2d/%d] %-18s %4d trials %4d units  %5.1fs  (elapsed %5.1fs, total ~%5.1fs)'\n   553→              % (i, len(names), name, res['n_trials'], res['n_units'],\n   554→                 time.time() - Hz, mean over the window, as in the paper\nRATE_SD_MS = 14                  # sigma of gausswin(15) in the reference, two sided here\nBRAIN_REGION = 'ALM'             # every recording targets ALM\n\nOUTCOME = {'incorrect': 0, 'correct': 1, 'ignore': 2}\nCONTEXT = {'WC': 0, 'DR': 1}\nLICK = {'left': 0, 'right': 1, 'no lick': 2}\nMOVEMENT = {'below threshold': 0, 'above threshold': 1, 'not visible': 2}\n\nINPUT_NAMES = ['time_from_go_cue']\nOUTPUT_NAMES = ['lick_direction', 'context', 'outcome',\n                'tongue_velocity', 'paw_velocity', 'motion_energy']\nOUTPUT_VALUES = [list(LICK), list(CONTEXT), list(OUTCOME),\n                 list(MOVEMENT), list(MOVEMENT), list(MOVEMENT)]\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------ loading --\n# Data structure files come in two MATLAB formats and motion energy in three layouts,\n# so both readers are needed and the motion energy wrapper is unwrapped until the\n# per-trial array appears.\n\ndef _h5(f, node):\n    \"\"\"Recursively read an h5py node from a MATLAB v7.3 file.\"\"\"\n    if isinstance(node, h5py.Group):                        # struct\n        return {k: _h5(f, node[k]) for k in node}\n    if node.attrs.get('MATLAB_class', b'').decode() == 'char':\n        return ''.join(chr(c) for c in np.asarray(node[()]).ravel())\n    if node.dtype == object:                                # cell array / struct-array field\n        return [_h5(f, f[r]) for r in np.asarray(node[()]).ravel()]\n    return np.asarray(node[()]).squeeze()\n\n\ndef load_mat(path):\n    \"\"\"Load a data_structure .mat as nested dicts (files are either v7.3 HDF5 or v5).\"\"\"\n    try:\n        with h5py.File(path, 'r') as f:\n            return _h5(f, f['obj'])\n    except OSError:\n        return scipy.io.loadmat(path, simplify_cells=True)['obj']\n\n\ndef session_file(name, prefix, datadir):\n    \"\"\"Path to the data_structure or motionEnergy file of a session.\n\n    name    session name, e.g. 'EKH1_2021-08-07'\n    prefix  file kind, 'data_structure' or 'motionEnergy'\n    datadir directory holding the EPHYS sub-folders\n    \"\"\"\n    for folder in EPHYS:\n        path = os.path.join(datadir, folder, '%s_%s.mat' % (prefix, name))\n        if os.path.exists(path):\n            return path\n    raise FileNotFoundError('%s_%s.mat' % (prefix, name))\n\n\ndef load_motion_energy(name, datadir):\n    \"\"\"Motion energy of each trial, one value per camera frame.\"\"\"\n    me = scipy.io.loadmat(session_file(name, 'motionEnergy', datadir), simplify_cells=True)['me']\n    while isinstance(me, dict):              # most files wrap it once, three wrap it twice\n        me = me['data']\n    return me\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------- trials --\n# One row per trial with the three per-trial outputs. Early-lick and photostim trials\n# are dropped, following the paper. The index keeps the original trial numbers, which\n# every later step uses to reach back into the raw arrays.\n\ndef trial_column(bp, *names):\n    \"\"\"One entry per trial from a field of bp, e.g. trial_column(bp, 'stim', 'enable').\"\"\"\n    n_trials = int(np.ravel(bp['Ntrials'])[0])\n    field = bp\n    for name in names:\n        field = field[name]\n    return np.ravel(np.asarray(field, float))[:n_trials]\n\n\ndef trial_info(bp):\n    \"\"\"Outcome, context, and lick direction per trial, without early-lick or photostim trials.\"\"\"\n    hit = trial_column(bp, 'hit') > 0\n    miss = trial_column(bp, 'miss') > 0\n    right = trial_column(bp, 'R') > 0\n    autowater = trial_column(bp, 'autowater') > 0\n    early = trial_column(bp, 'early') > 0\n    stim = trial_column(bp, 'stim', 'enable') > 0\n    n_trials = hit.size\n\n    # a hit is a correct lick and a miss an incorrect one; the animal ignored the rest\n    outcome = np.full(n_trials, OUTCOME['ignore'])\n    outcome[hit] = OUTCOME['correct']\n    outcome[miss] = OUTCOME['incorrect']\n\n    # water is given from a random port without any cue in the WC context\n    context = np.where(autowater, CONTEXT['WC'], CONTEXT['DR'])\n\n    # a hit licks the instructed port, a miss licks the other one, an ignore neither\n    lick_direction = np.full(n_trials, LICK['no lick'])\n    lick_direction[hit] = np.where(right[hit], LICK['right'], LICK['left'])\n    lick_direction[miss] = np.where(right[miss], LICK['left'], LICK['right'])\n\n    info = pd.DataFrame({'lick_direction': lick_direction, 'context': context,\n                         'outcome': outcome})\n    info.index.name = 'trial'\n    return info[~early & ~stim]\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------- camera --\n# Tongue velocity, paw velocity, and motion energy for one session. A class because the\n# video offset, the per-view scales, and the median split are session-wide\n# quantities that every trial reuses.\n\ndef _feature(traj, index):\n    \"\"\"x, y, and likelihood of one feature, whichever way the file orders ts.\"\"\"\n    ts = np.asarray(traj['ts'])\n    if ts.shape[0] == len(traj['featNames']):      # v7.3 reads it as (features, xyl, frames)\n        return ts[index]\n    return ts[:, :, index].T                       # v5 keeps MATLAB's (frames, xyl, features)\n\n\ndef _runs(valid):\n    \"\"\"First and last+1 index of each contiguous run of valid frames.\"\"\"\n    edges = np.diff(np.concatenate([[0], valid.astype(int), [0]]))\n    return zip(np.flatnonzero(edges == 1), np.flatnonzero(edges == -1))\n\n\ndef _bin_frames(time, values):\n    \"\"\"Mean of the valid frames in each bin, NaN where a bin holds no valid frame.\"\"\"\n    bin_of_frame = np.searchsorted(BIN_EDGES, time, side='right') - 1\n    valid = np.isfinite(values) & (bin_of_frame >= 0) & (bin_of_frame < N_BINS)\n    total = np.bincount(bin_of_frame[valid], weights=values[valid], minlength=N_BINS)\n    count = np.bincount(bin_of_frame[valid], minlength=N_BINS)\n    return np.where(count > 0, total / np.maximum(count, 1), np.nan)\n\n\ndef _mean_over_available(first, second):\n    \"\"\"Mean of the two, whichever one is there alone, or NaN where neither is.\"\"\"\n    stacked = np.stack([first, second])\n    count = np.sum(np.isfinite(stacked), axis=0)\n    total = np.nansum(stacked, axis=0)\n    return np.where(count > 0, total / np.maximum(count, 1), np.nan)\n\n\ndef _discretize(values, threshold):\n    \"\"\"Two classes split at the threshold; untracked bins take the trailing class.\"\"\"\n    code = np.where(values >= threshold, MOVEMENT['above threshold'], MOVEMENT['below threshold'])\n    return np.where(np.isnan(values), MOVEMENT['not visible'], code)\n\n\nclass Camera:\n    \"\"\"The three camera streams of a session, binned and cut into classes.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, obj, name, trials, datadir):\n        self.obj = obj\n        self.trials = list(trials)\n        self.go_cue = trial_column(obj['bp'], 'ev', 'goCue')\n        self.offset = self._video_offset()             # one constant for the whole session\n        self.energy = load_motion_energy(name, datadir)\n\n        # frame resolution velocity, for the two tongue views and for the paw\n        self.side_tongue = self._trial_velocity(SIDE_TONGUE)\n        self.bottom_tongue = self._trial_velocity(BOTTOM_TONGUE)\n        self.paw = self._trial_velocity(PAW)\n\n        # the two cameras see the tongue at different pixel scales\n        self.side_scale = self._view_scale(self.side_tongue)\n        self.bottom_scale = self._view_scale(self.bottom_tongue)\n\n    def _trial_velocity(self, feature):\n        \"\"\"Frame resolution velocity of one tracked feature, one entry per trial.\"\"\"\n        return {trial: self._velocity(self._track(trial, feature)) for trial in self.trials}\n\n    def _video_offset(self):\n        \"\"\"Seconds by which the video clock leads the behavior clock (findVideoOffset.m).\"\"\"\n        sample_rate = float(np.ravel(self.obj['sglx']['fs'])[0])\n        video_start = np.ravel(self.obj['sglx']['bitcode']['bitstart']) / sample_rate\n        behavior_start = trial_column(self.obj['bp'], 'ev', 'bitStart')\n        return pd.Series(video_start).mode().iloc[0] - pd.Series(behavior_start).mode().iloc[0]\n\n    def _traj(self, trial, view):\n        \"\"\"Tracking of one trial and camera, however the file stores it.\"\"\"\n        traj = self.obj['traj'][view]\n        if isinstance(traj, dict):                     # v7.3: one entry per camera\n            return {field: traj[field][trial] for field in traj}\n        entry = traj[trial]                            # v5: one entry per trial\n        if hasattr(entry, '_fieldnames'):              # scipy leaves nested structs as objects\n            return {field: getattr(entry, field) for field in entry._fieldnames}\n        return entry\n\n    # camera frame time after correcting for the video offset and aligning to the go cue, in seconds\n    def _frame_time(self, trial, view):\n        \"\"\"Frame times of one trial and camera, in seconds from go cue onset.\"\"\"\n        frame_times = np.ravel(self._traj(trial, view)['frameTimes'])\n        return frame_times - self.offset - self.go_cue[trial]\n\n    def _camera_of(self, trial, feature):\n        \"\"\"The camera that tracks a feature, and its tracking for this trial.\"\"\"\n        for view in (SIDE, BOTTOM):\n            traj = self._traj(trial, view)\n            if feature in list(traj['featNames']):     # the name picks out the camera\n                return view, traj\n        raise KeyError('%s is tracked by neither camera' % feature)\n\n    # x, y, and likelihood within the time window\n    def _track(self, trial, feature):\n        \"\"\"x, y, and likelihood of one feature over the frames inside the window.\"\"\"\n        view, traj = self._camera_of(trial, feature)\n        x, y, likelihood = _feature(traj, list(traj['featNames']).index(feature))\n        time = self._frame_time(trial, view)           # frame counts can differ between views\n        inside = (time >= T_START) & (time <= T_STOP)\n        return time[inside], x[inside], y[inside], likelihood[inside]\n\n    # smooth the x, y postion, and compute the speed, filter by the likelihood threshold > 0.90\n    def _velocity(self, track):\n        \"\"\"Combined velocity over the valid frames, NaN elsewhere.\"\"\"\n        time, x, y, likelihood = track\n        speed = np.full(time.size, np.nan)\n        if time.size < 2:                              # a few trials have no frame times\n            return time, speed\n\n        sigma = SMOOTH_SIGMA_MS / 1000 / np.median(np.diff(time))\n        valid = likelihood > LIKELIHOOD_CUT            # x and y are NaN below this\n\n        # smooth and differentiate inside each run of tracked frames, never across a gap\n        for start, stop in _runs(valid):\n            if stop - start < 2:                       # a lone frame has no velocity\n                continue\n            sx = gaussian_filter1d(x[start:stop], sigma, mode='mirror')\n            sy = gaussian_filter1d(y[start:stop], sigma, mode='mirror')\n            speed[start:stop] = np.hypot(np.gradient(sx, time[start:stop]),\n                                         np.gradient(sy, time[start:stop]))\n        return time, speed\n\n    def _view_scale(self, velocity):\n        \"\"\"The percentile that puts one camera's velocity on a comparable scale.\"\"\"\n        pooled = np.concatenate([velocity[trial][1] for trial in self.trials])\n        return np.nanpercentile(pooled, NORMALIZE_PCT)\n\n    # the tongue is seen by both cameras, on different pixel scales, so each view is\n    # binned and divided by its own percentile before the two are averaged\n    def tongue_velocity(self):\n        \"\"\"Binned tongue velocity, as (n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n        side = np.array([_bin_frames(*self.side_tongue[trial]) for trial in self.trials])\n        bottom = np.array([_bin_frames(*self.bottom_tongue[trial]) for trial in self.trials])\n        side = side / self.side_scale\n        bottom = bottom / self.bottom_scale\n        return _mean_over_available(side, bottom)      # one view alone where the other is missing\n\n    # only one paw stays tracked through the delay, so it is used on its own, in pixels\n    def paw_velocity(self):\n        \"\"\"Binned paw velocity, as (n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n        return np.array([_bin_frames(*self.paw[trial]) for trial in self.trials])\n\n    # motion energy is already one number per frame, so it only needs binning\n    def motion_energy(self):\n        \"\"\"Binned motion energy, as (n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n        rows = []\n        for trial in self.trials:\n            time = self._frame_time(trial, SIDE)       # motion energy follows the side camera\n            inside = (time >= T_START) & (time <= T_STOP)\n            rows.append(_bin_frames(time[inside], self.energy[trial][inside]))\n        return np.array(rows)\n\n    def outputs(self):\n        \"\"\"The three streams, each cut in two at its own session median.\"\"\"\n        tongue = self.tongue_velocity()\n        paw = self.paw_velocity()\n        energy = self.motion_energy()\n        return (_discretize(tongue, np.nanpercentile(tongue, SPLIT_PCT)),\n                _discretize(paw, np.nanpercentile(paw, SPLIT_PCT)),\n                _discretize(energy, np.nanpercentile(energy, SPLIT_PCT)))\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------- neural --\n# Spike times are already on the behavior clock and relative to trial start, so aligning\n# to the go cue is one subtraction. Quality is a free text label written with either case\n# and with a few typos, so it is matched in lower case.\n\ndef _quality(cluster):\n    \"\"\"Quality label of a cluster; a handful carry no label at all.\"\"\"\n    label = cluster['quality']\n    return label.strip().lower() if isinstance(label, str) else ''\n\n\nclass Neural:\n    \"\"\"The QC-passing units of a session, as smoothed firing rates.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, obj, probes, trials):\n        self.obj = obj\n        self.go_cue = trial_column(obj['bp'], 'ev', 'goCue')\n        self.clusters = [cluster for probe in probes                # probes concatenated\n                         for cluster in self._probe_clusters(probe)\n                         if _quality(cluster) not in QUALITY_DROP]\n\n        # two sessions keep running after the recording stops, leaving trials with no spikes\n        last = max(int(np.asarray(cluster['trial'], int).max(initial=0))\n                   for cluster in self.clusters)\n        self.trials = np.asarray([t for t in trials if t < last])   # trial numbers are 1 based\n\n    def _probe_clusters(self, probe):\n        \"\"\"One dict per cluster on the given probe, however the file stores them.\"\"\"\n        entry = self.obj['clu'][probe - 1]\n        if isinstance(entry, dict) and isinstance(entry['tm'], list):\n            # v7.3: one entry per probe, each field holding one array per cluster\n            return [{field: entry[field][i] for field in entry}\n                    for i in range(len(entry['tm']))]\n        return self.obj['clu']                     # v5: one entry per cluster, single probe\n\n    def spike_count(self, cluster):\n        \"\"\"Spikes of one cluster per trial and bin, aligned to the go cue.\"\"\"\n        spike_trial = np.asarray(cluster['trial'], int) - 1         # trial numbers are 1 based\n        spike_time = np.asarray(cluster['trialtm'], float) - self.go_cue[spike_trial]\n\n        # count spikes into a trial by bin grid; those outside the window fall off the edges\n        trial_edges = np.arange(self.go_cue.size + 1) - 0.5\n        counts, _, _ = np.histogram2d(spike_trial, spike_time, bins=[trial_edges, BIN_EDGES])\n        return counts[self.trials]                                  # only the trials we keep\n\n    def rates(self):\n        \"\"\"Firing rate of every unit above MIN_RATE, as (n_units, n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n        sigma = RATE_SD_MS / 1000 / BIN\n        rates = []\n        for cluster in self.clusters:\n            rate = self.spike_count(cluster) / BIN                 # counts -> Hz\n            if rate.mean() > MIN_RATE:\n                rates.append(gaussian_filter1d(rate, sigma, axis=1, mode='reflect'))\n        return np.asarray(rates, np.float32)\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------ session --\n\ndef process_session(name, datadir):\n    \"\"\"Convert one session into per-trial neural, input, and output arrays.\"\"\"\n    obj = load_mat(session_file(name, 'data_structure', datadir))\n    info = trial_info(obj['bp'])\n\n    neural = Neural(obj, SESSIONS[name], info.index)\n    info = info.loc[neural.trials]                 # drops any trial past the recording\n    n_trials = len(info)\n\n    rates = neural.rates()\n    tongue, paw, energy = Camera(obj, name, info.index, datadir).outputs()\n\n    # the three per-trial values are repeated across bins so all six outputs share one array\n    out = np.empty((n_trials, len(OUTPUT_NAMES), N_BINS), np.int8)\n    out[:, 0, :] = info['lick_direction'].to_numpy()[:, None]\n    out[:, 1, :] = info['context'].to_numpy()[:, None]\n    out[:, 2, :] = info['outcome'].to_numpy()[:, None]\n    out[:, 3, :] = tongue\n    out[:, 4, :] = paw\n    out[:, 5, :] = energy\n\n    inp = np.tile(TIME.astype(np.float32), (n_trials, 1, 1))       # (n_trials, 1, N_BINS)\n\n    return {\n        'session_id': name,\n        'subject': name.split('_')[0],\n        'neural': [np.ascontiguousarray(rates[:, t, :]) for t in range(n_trials)],\n        'input': [inp[t] for t in range(n_trials)],\n        'output': [out[t] for t in range(n_trials)],\n        'n_trials': n_trials,\n        'n_units': int(rates.shape[0]),\n    }\n\n\ndef assemble(results):\n    \"\"\"Stitch per-session results into the target dictionary.\"\"\"\n    subjects = sorted({r['subject'] for r in results})\n    sub_ix = {s: i for i, s in enumerate(subjects)}\n\n    return {\n        'neural': [r['neural'] for r in results],\n        'input': [r['input'] for r in results],\n        'output': [r['output'] for r in results],\n\n        'subjects': subjects,\n        'subject_idx': np.array([sub_ix[r['subject']] for r in results], np.int32),\n\n        'brain_regions': [BRAIN_REGION],\n        'brain_region_idx': [np.zeros(r['n_units'], np.int32) for r in results],\n\n        'input_names': INPUT_NAMES,\n        'output_names': OUTPUT_NAMES,\n        'output_values': OUTPUT_VALUES,\n\n        'metadata': {\n            'task_description':\n                'Head-fixed mice lick left or right for water. Handle boundary conditions\n   176→    if bctype == 'reflect':\n   177→        x_filt = np.concatenate([x[:N, :], x], axis=0)\n   178→        trim = N\n   179→    elif bctype == 'zeropad':\n   180→        x_filt = np.concatenate([np.zeros((N, x.shape[1])), x], axis=0)\n   181→        trim = N\n   182→    else:\n   183→        x_filt = x\n   184→        trim = 0\n   185→\n   186→    # Convolve each column\n   187→    out = np.zeros_like(x_filt)\n   188→    for j in range(x_filt.shape[1]):\n   189→        out[:, j] = np.convolve(x_filt[:, j], kern, mode='same')\n   190→    out = out[trim:, :]\n   191→\n   192→    if was_1d:\n   193→        return out[:, 0]\n   194→    return out\n   195→\n   196→\n   197→# =============================================================================\n   198→# Data loading functions\n   199→# =============================================================================\n   200→\n   201→def _detect_file_format(data_fn):\n   202→    \"\"\"Detect if a .mat file is HDF5 (v7.3) or v5 format.\"\"\"\n   203→    try:\n   204→        f = h5py.File(data_fn, 'r')\n   205→        f.close()\n   206→        return 'hdf5'\n   207→    except Exception:\n   208→        return 'v5'\n   209→\n   210→\n   211→def _load_raw_data_v5(data_fn, probes):\n   212→    \"\"\"Load raw data from MATLAB v5 format file. Returns common dict format.\"\"\"\n   213→    import scipy.io as sio\n   214→    mat = sio.loadmat(data_fn, squeeze_me=False)\n   215→    obj = mat['obj']\n   216→\n   217→    # --- bp ---\n   218→    bp_raw = obj['bp'][0, 0]\n   219→    bp = {}\n   220→    bp['Ntrials'] = int(bp_raw['Ntrials'][0, 0].flat[0])\n   221→    ntrials = bp['Ntrials']\n   222→\n   223→    for field in ['hit', 'miss', 'no', 'early', 'L', 'R', 'autowater']:\n   224→        bp[field] = bp_raw[field][0, 0].flatten().astype(np.float64)[:ntrials]\n   225→\n   226→    stim_enable = np.zeros(ntrials)\n   227→    if 'stim' in bp_raw.dtype.names:\n   228→        stim_raw = bp_raw['stim'][0, 0]\n   229→        if 'enable' in stim_raw.dtype.names:\n   230→            stim_enable = stim_raw['enable'][0, 0].flatten().astype(np.float64)[:ntrials]\n   231→    bp['stim_enable'] = stim_enable\n   232→\n   233→    ev = {}\n   234→    ev_raw = bp_raw['ev'][0, 0]\n   235→    for field in ['goCue', 'sample', 'delay']:\n   236→        ev[field] = ev_raw[field][0, 0].flatten().astype(np.float64)[:ntrials]\n   237→    bp['ev'] = ev\n   238→\n   239→    # --- vidshift ---\n   240→    vidshift = 0.5\n   241→    try:\n   242→        bit_start_arr = ev_raw['bitStart'][0, 0].flatten().astype(np.float64)\n   243→        bit_start = np.nanmedian(bit_start_arr)\n   244→        sglx_raw = obj['sglx'][0, 0]\n   245→        fs = sglx_raw['fs'][0, 0].flat[0]\n   246→        bitcode_raw = sglx_raw['bitcode'][0, 0]\n   247→        bitcode_bitstart = np.nanmedian(bitcode_raw['bitstart'][0, 0].flatten().astype(np.float64))\n   248→        vid_file_offset = bitcode_bitstart / fs\n   249→        vidshift = vid_file_offset - bit_start\n   250→    except Exception:\n   251→        pass\n   252→\n   253→    # --- clu ---\n   254→    clu_raw = obj['clu'][0, 0]\n   255→    all_spike_times = []\n   256→    all_spike_trials = []\n   257→    all_qualities = []\n   258→\n   259→    for probe_num in probes:\n   260→        probe_idx = probe_num - 1\n   261→        probe_data = clu_raw[0, probe_idx]\n   262→        n_units = probe_data.shape[1] if probe_data.ndim > 1 else probe_data.shape[0]\n   263→        for i in range(n_units):\n   264→            unit = probe_data[0, i] if probe_data.ndim > 1 else probe_data[i]\n   265→            quality = str(unit['quality'].flat[0]).strip().lower() if unit['quality'].size > 0 else ''\n   266→            trialtm = unit['trialtm'].flatten().astype(np.float64)\n   267→            trial = unit['trial'].flatten().astype(np.float64)\n   268→            all_qualities.append(quality)\n   269→            all_spike_times.append(trialtm)\n   270→            all_spike_trials.append(trial)\n   271→\n   272→    # --- traj (for velocity computation) ---\n   273→    traj_data = None\n   274→    try:\n   275→        traj_raw = obj['traj'][0, 0]\n   276→        traj_data = {'views': []}\n   277→        for view_idx in range(traj_raw.shape[1]):\n   278→            view_data = traj_raw[0, view_idx]\n   279→            view_trials = []\n   280→            feat_names = None\n   281→            for t in range(min(ntrials, view_data.shape[1] if view_data.ndim > 1 else view_data.shape[0])):\n   282→                trial_data = view_data[0, t] if view_data.ndim > 1 else view_data[t]\n   283→                ts = trial_data['ts'].astype(np.float64)  # (nFrames, 3, nFeats) in v5\n   284→                ft = trial_data['frameTimes'].flatten().astype(np.float64)\n   285→                if feat_names is None:\n   286→                    fn_raw = trial_data['featNames'].flatten()\n   287→                    feat_names = [str(x.flat[0]).strip() for x in fn_raw]\n   288→                view_trials.append({'ts': ts, 'frameTimes': ft, 'ts_format': 'v5'})\n   289→            traj_data['views'].append({'trials': view_trials, 'feat_names': feat_names})\n   290→    except Exception:\n   291→        pass\n   292→\n   293→    return {\n   294→        'bp': bp, 'vidshift': vidshift, 'ntrials': ntrials,\n   295→        'all_spike_times': all_spike_times, 'all_spike_trials': all_spike_trials,\n   296→        'all_qualities': all_qualities, 'traj_data': traj_data,\n   297→        'n_units_raw': len(all_qualities),\n   298→    }\n   299→\n   300→\n   301→def _load_raw_data_hdf5(data_fn, probes):\n   302→    \"\"\"Load raw data from HDF5 (v7.3) format file. Returns common dict format.\"\"\"\n   303→    f = h5py.File(data_fn, 'r')\n   304→    obj = f['obj']\n   305→\n   306→    bp = {}\n   307→    bp_group = obj['bp']\n   308→    bp['Ntrials'] = int(bp_group['Ntrials'][()].flat[0])\n   309→    ntrials = bp['Ntrials']\n   310→\n   311→    for field in ['hit', 'miss', 'no', 'early', 'L', 'R', 'autowater']:\n   312→        bp[field] = bp_group[field][()].flatten().astype(np.float64)[:ntrials]\n   313→\n   314→    stim_enable = np.zeros(ntrials)\n   315→    if 'stim' in bp_group:\n   316→        stim_grp = bp_group['stim']\n   317→        if 'enable' in stim_grp:\n   318→            stim_enable = stim_grp['enable'][()].flatten().astype(np.float64)[:ntrials]\n   319→    bp['stim_enable'] = stim_enable\n   320→\n   321→    ev = {}\n   322→    ev_group = bp_group['ev']\n   323→    for field in ['goCue', 'sample', 'delay']:\n   324→        ev[field] = ev_group[field][()].flatten().astype(np.float64)[:ntrials]\n   325→    bp['ev'] = ev\n   326→\n   327→    vidshift = 0.5\n   328→    try:\n   329→        bit_start = np.nanmedian(bp_group['ev']['bitStart'][()].flatten())\n   330→        sglx = obj['sglx']\n   331→        fs = sglx['fs'][()].flat[0]\n   332→        bitcode_bitstart = np.nanmedian(sglx['bitcode']['bitstart'][()].flatten())\n   333→        vid_file_offset = bitcode_bitstart / fs\n   334→        vidshift = vid_file_offset - bit_start\n   335→    except Exception:\n   336→        pass\n   337→\n   338→    clu_refs = obj['clu'][()].flatten()\n   339→    all_spike_times = []\n   340→    all_spike_trials = []\n   341→    all_qualities = []\n   342→\n   343→    for probe_num in probes:\n   344→        probe_idx = probe_num - 1\n   345→        probe_ref = clu_refs[probe_idx]\n   346→        probe_group = f[probe_ref]\n   347→        quality_refs = probe_group['quality'][()].flatten()\n   348→        trialtm_refs = probe_group['trialtm'][()].flatten()\n   349→        trial_refs = probe_group['trial'][()].flatten()\n   350→\n   351→        for i in range(len(quality_refs)):\n   352→            quality = h5_read_string(f, quality_refs[i]).strip().lower()\n   353→            trialtm = f[trialtm_refs[i]][()].flatten().astype(np.float64)\n   354→            trial = f[trial_refs[i]][()].flatten().astype(np.float64)\n   355→            all_qualities.append(quality)\n   356→            all_spike_times.append(trialtm)\n   357→            all_spike_trials.append(trial)\n   358→\n   359→    # --- traj ---\n   360→    traj_data = None\n   361→    try:\n   362→        traj_refs = obj['traj'][()].flatten()\n   363→        traj_data = {'views': []}\n   364→        for view_idx in range(len(traj_refs)):\n   365→            traj_group = f[traj_refs[view_idx]]\n   366→            ts_refs = traj_group['ts'][()].flatten()\n   367→            ft_refs = traj_group['frameTimes'][()].flatten()\n   368→            feat_names_refs = traj_group['featNames'][()].flatten()\n   369→\n   370→            # Read feat names from first trial\n   371→            feat_refs_trial = f[feat_names_refs[0]][()].flatten()\n   372→            feat_names = [h5_read_string(f, fr) for fr in feat_refs_trial]\n   373→\n   374→            view_trials = []\n   375→            for t in range(min(ntrials, len(ts_refs))):\n   376→                try:\n   377→                    ts = f[ts_refs[t]][()].astype(np.float64)  # (nFeats, 3, nFrames) in HDF5\n   378→                    ft = f[ft_refs[t]][()].flatten().astype(np.float64)\n   379→                    view_trials.append({'ts': ts, 'frameTimes': ft, 'ts_format': 'hdf5'})\n   380→                except Exception:\n   381→                    view_trials.append(None)\n   382→            traj_data['views'].append({'trials': view_trials, 'feat_names': feat_names})\n   383→    except Exception:\n   384→        pass\n   385→\n   386→    f.close()\n   387→\n   388→    return {\n   389→        'bp': bp, 'vidshift': vidshift, 'ntrials': ntrials,\n   390→        'all_spike_times': all_spike_times, 'all_spike_trials': all_spike_trials,\n   391→        'all_qualities': all_qualities, 'traj_data': traj_data,\n   392→        'n_units_raw': len(all_qualities),\n   393→    }\n   394→\n   395→\n   396→def load_session(anm, date, probes, data_dir_key, params):\n   397→    \"\"\"\n   398→    Load and process one session, following the reference pipeline:\n   399→    loadObjs -> findClusters -> alignSpikes -> getSeq -> removeLowFRClusters\n   400→    Also loads motion energy and computes kinematic features.\n   401→    \"\"\"\n   402→    t0 = time.time()\n   403→    data_dir = DATA_DIRS[data_dir_key]\n   404→    data_fn = os.path.join(data_dir, f'data_structure_{anm}_{date}.mat')\n   405→    me_fn = os.path.join(data_dir, f'motionEnergy_{anm}_{date}.mat')\n   406→\n   407→    session_id = f'{anm}_{date}'\n   408→\n   409→    if not os.path.exists(data_fn):\n   410→        print(f\"  WARNING: {data_fn} not found, skipping\")\n   411→        return None\n   412→\n   413→    # Detect format and load raw data\n   414→    fmt = _detect_file_format(data_fn)\n   415→    try:\n   416→        if fmt == 'hdf5':\n   417→            raw = _load_raw_data_hdf5(data_fn, probes)\n   418→        else:\n   419→            raw = _load_raw_data_v5(data_fn, probes)\n   420→    except Exception as e:\n   421→        print(f\"  WARNING: Could not load {session_id}: {e}\")\n   422→        return None\n   423→\n   424→    bp = raw['bp']\n   425→    ntrials = raw['ntrials']\n   426→    vidshift = raw['vidshift']\n   427→    all_spike_times = raw['all_spike_times']\n   428→    all_spike_trials = raw['all_spike_trials']\n   429→    all_qualities = raw['all_qualities']\n   430→    traj_data = raw['traj_data']\n   431→    n_units_before_quality = raw['n_units_raw']\n   432→    ev = bp['ev']\n   433→\n   434→    # --- Filter clusters by quality ---\n   435→    quality_exclude = params['quality_exclude']\n   436→    keep_mask = np.array([q not in quality_exclude and q != '' and q != 'nan'\n   437→                          for q in all_qualities])\n   438→    good_indices = np.where(keep_mask)[0]\n   439→\n   440→    if len(good_indices) == 0:\n   441→        print(f\"  WARNING: {session_id} has no units after quality filter, skipping\")\n   442→        return None\n   443→\n   444→    # --- Trial filtering ---\n   445→    trial_mask = (bp['early'] == 0) & (bp['stim_enable'] == 0)\n   446→    valid_trials = np.where(trial_mask)[0] + 1  # 1-indexed\n   447→    if len(valid_trials) < 2:\n   448→        print(f\"  WARNING: {session_id} has < 2 valid trials, skipping\")\n   449→        return None\n   450→\n   451→    # --- Align spikes to goCue and bin ---\n   452→    align_times = ev[params['align_event']]\n   453→    edges = np.arange(params['tmin'], params['tmax'] + params['dt'], params['dt'])\n   454→    time_axis = edges[:-1] + params['dt'] / 2\n   455→    n_timebins = len(time_axis)\n   456→    n_valid_trials = len(valid_trials)\n   457→\n   458→    trialdat = np.zeros((len(good_indices), n_timebins, n_valid_trials), dtype=np.float32)\n   459→\n   460→    for neuron_idx, clu_idx in enumerate(good_indices):\n   461→        spike_tm = all_spike_times[clu_idx]\n   462→        spike_trial = all_spike_trials[clu_idx]\n   463→\n   464→        for t_idx, trial_num in enumerate(valid_trials):\n   465→            spk_mask = spike_trial == trial_num\n   466→            if not np.any(spk_mask):\n   467→                continue\n   468→            spk_times = spike_tm[spk_mask] - align_times[trial_num - 1]\n   469→            counts, _ = np.histogram(spk_times, bins=edges)\n   470→            fr = causal_gaussian_smooth(counts.astype(np.float64) / params['dt'],\n   471→                                       params['smooth_window'],\n   472→                                       params['smooth_bctype'])\n   473→            trialdat[neuron_idx, :, t_idx] = fr.astype(np.float32)\n   474→\n   475→    # --- Remove low FR clusters ---\n   476→    mean_fr = np.mean(trialdat, axis=(1, 2))\n   477→    fr_mask = mean_fr > params['low_fr']\n   478→    trialdat = trialdat[fr_mask, :, :]\n   479→    n_neurons = trialdat.shape[0]\n   480→\n   481→    if n_neurons < params['min_units']:\n   482→        print(f\"  WARNING: {session_id} has {n_neurons} units (< {params['min_units']}), skipping\")\n   483→        return None\n   484→\n   485→    # --- Extract trial-level variables ---\n   486→    valid_trial_indices = valid_trials - 1\n   487→    lick_direction = bp['R'][valid_trial_indices].copy()\n   488→    behavioral_context = 1.0 - bp['autowater'][valid_trial_indices]\n   489→    outcome = bp['hit'][valid_trial_indices].copy()\n   490→\n   491→    # --- Load Motion Energy ---\n   492→    me_data = None\n   493→    if os.path.exists(me_fn):\n   494→        try:\n   495→            me_data = _load_motion_energy_generic(me_fn, traj_data, vidshift,\n   496→                                                  align_times, time_axis, ntrials)\n   497→        except Exception as e:\n   498→            print(f\"  WARNING: Could not load motion energy for {session_id}: {e}\")\n   499→\n   500→    # --- Compute tongue and paw velocity from DLC ---\n   501→    tongue_vel = _compute_feature_velocity_generic(\n   502→        traj_data, ntrials, view=2, feat_name='top_tongue',\n   503→        vidshift=vidshift, align_times=align_times,\n   504→        time_axis=time_axis, is_tongue=True)\n   505→\n   506→    paw_vel = _compute_feature_velocity_generic(\n   507→        traj_data, ntrials, view=2, feat_name='top_paw',\n   508→        vidshift=vidshift, align_times=align_times,\n   509→        time_axis=time_axis, is_tongue=False)\n   510→\n   511→    # --- Subsample to valid trials ---\n   512→    valid_0idx = valid_trial_indices  # 0-indexed\n   513→\n   514→    if tongue_vel is not None:\n   515→        tongue_vel = tongue_vel[:, valid_0idx]\n   516→    if paw_vel is not None:\n   517→        paw_vel = paw_vel[:, valid_0idx]\n   518→    if me_data is not None:\n   519→        me_data = me_data[:, valid_0idx]\n   520→\n   521→    # --- Discretize continuous outputs (50th percentile per session) ---\n   522→    tongue_vel_disc = _discretize_velocity(tongue_vel, n_timebins, n_valid_trials)\n   523→    paw_vel_disc = _discretize_velocity(paw_vel, n_timebins, n_valid_trials)\n   524→    me_disc = _discretize_velocity(me_data, n_timebins, n_valid_trials)\n   525→\n   526→    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n   527→    print(f\"  {session_id}: {n_neurons} neurons, {n_valid_trials} trials, \"\n   528→          f\"{n_units_before_quality} raw units -> {int(keep_mask.sum())} after quality -> \"\n   529→          f\"{n_neurons} after FR filter ({elapsed:.1f}s)\")\n   530→\n   531→    return {\n   532→        'session_id': session_id,\n   533→        'animal': anm,\n   534→        'date': date,\n   535→        'n_neurons': n_neurons,\n   536→        'n_trials': n_valid_trials,\n   537→        'time_axis': time_axis,\n   538→        'trialdat': trialdat,  # (n_neurons, n_timebins, n_trials)\n   539→        'lick_direction': lick_direction,\n   540→        'behavioral_context': behavioral_context,\n   541→        'outcome': outcome,\n   542→        'tongue_vel_disc': tongue_vel_disc,  # (n_timebins, n_trials)\n   543→        'paw_vel_disc': paw_vel_disc,\n   544→        'me_disc': me_disc,\n   545→        'tongue_vel_raw': tongue_vel,\n   546→        'paw_vel_raw': paw_vel,\n   547→        'me_raw': me_data,\n   548→    }\n   549→\n   550→\n   551→def _load_motion_energy_generic(me_fn, traj_data, vidshift, align_times, time_axis, ntrials):\n   552→    \"\"\"Load motion energy and align to neural time axis following loadMotionEnergy.m\"\"\"\n   553→    import scipy.io as sio\n   554→\n   555→    me_cell = None\n   556→    try:\n   557→        me_file = sio.loadmat(me_fn, squeeze_me=True)\n   558→        me_struct = me_file['me']\n   559→        me_cell = me_struct['data'].item()\n   560→    except Exception:\n   561→        me_h5 = h5py.File(me_fn, 'r')\n   562→        me_grp = me_h5['me']\n   563→        me_data_refs = me_grp['data'][()].flatten()\n   564→        me_cell = [me_h5[ref][()].flatten().astype(np.float64) for ref in me_data_refs]\n   565→        me_h5.close()\n   566→\n   567→    side_cam = traj_data['views'][0] if traj_data is not None else None\n   568→    n_time = len(time_axis)\n   569→    me_aligned = np.full((n_time, ntrials), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n   570→\n   571→    for trix in range(ntrials):\n   572→        try:\n   573→            me_trial = me_cell[trix].flatten().astype(np.float64) if hasattr(me_cell[trix], 'flatten') else np.array([float(me_cell[trix])])\n   574→        except (IndexError, TypeError):\n   575→            continue\n   576→        if me_trial.size == 0:\n   577→            continue\n   578→\n   579→        ft = None\n   580→        if side_cam is not None and trix < len(side_cam['trials']) and side_cam['trials'][trix] is not None:\n   581→            ft = side_cam['trials'][trix]['frameTimes']\n   582→        if ft is None or ft.size == 0 or np.all(np.isnan(ft)):\n   583→            ft = np.arange(1, me_trial.size + 1) / 400.0\n   584→\n   585→        ft_aligned = ft - vidshift - align_times[trix]\n   586→        min_len = min(len(ft_aligned), len(me_trial))\n   587→        ft_aligned, me_trial = ft_aligned[:min_len], me_trial[:min_len]\n   588→        valid = ~np.isnan(ft_aligned) & ~np.isnan(me_trial)\n   589→        if valid.sum() < 2:\n   590→            continue\n   591→        try:\n   592→            me_aligned[:, trix] = interp1d(ft_aligned[valid], me_trial[valid],\n   593→                                           kind='linear', bounds_error=False,\n   594→                                           fill_value=np.nan)(time_axis).astype(np.float32)\n   595→        except Exception:\n   596→            continue\n   597→\n   598→    for trix in range(ntrials):\n   599→        col = me_aligned[:, trix]\n   600→        if not np.isnan(col).all() and np.isnan(col).any():\n   601→            me_aligned[:, trix] = _fill_nearest(col)\n   602→\n   603→    return me_aligned\n   604→\n   605→\n   606→def _compute_feature_velocity_generic(traj_data, ntrials, view, feat_name, vidshift, align_times, time_axis, is_tongue=False):\n   607→    \"\"\"Compute velocity of a DLC feature from traj_data (format-agnostic).\"\"\"\n   608→    n_time = len(time_axis)\n   609→    speed = np.full((n_time, ntrials), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n   610→\n   611→    if traj_data is None:\n   612→        return None\n   613→\n   614→    view_idx = view - 1\n   615→    if view_idx >= len(traj_data['views']):\n   616→        return None\n   617→\n   618→    view_data = traj_data['views'][view_idx]\n   619→    feat_names = view_data['feat_names']\n   620→\n   621→    feat_idx = None\n   622→    for idx, fn in enumerate(feat_names):\n   623→        if fn == feat_name:\n   624→            feat_idx = idx\n   625→            break\n   626→    if feat_idx is None:\n   627→        for idx, fn in enumerate(feat_names):\n   628→            if feat_name in fn:\n   629→                feat_idx = idx\n   630→                break\n   631→    if feat_idx is None:\n   632→        return None\n   633→\n   634→    for trix in range(min(ntrials, len(view_data['trials']))):\n   635→        trial_info = view_data['trials'][trix]\n   636→        if trial_info is None:\n   637→            continue\n   638→        try:\n   639→            ts = trial_info['ts']\n   640→            ft = trial_info['frameTimes']\n   641→            ts_fmt = trial_info['ts_format']\n   642→\n   643→            if ts.ndim != 3:\n   644→                continue\n   645→\n   646→            if ts_fmt == 'hdf5':\n   647→                x_raw = ts[feat_idx, 0, :]\n   648→                y_raw = ts[feat_idx, 1, :]\n   649→            else:\n   650→                x_raw = ts[:, 0, feat_idx]\n   651→                y_raw = ts[:, 1, feat_idx]\n   652→\n   653→            if ft.size == 0 or np.all(np.isnan(ft)):\n   654→                ft = np.arange(1, len(x_raw) + 1) / 400.0\n   655→\n   656→            ft_aligned = ft - vidshift - align_times[trix]\n   657→            min_len = min(len(ft_aligned), len(x_raw))\n   658→            ft_a, x_r, y_r = ft_aligned[:min_len], x_raw[:min_len], y_raw[:min_len]\n   659→\n   660→            valid = ~np.isnan(ft_a) & ~np.isnan(x_r) & ~np.isnan(y_r)\n   661→            if valid.sum() < 2:\n   662→                continue\n   663→\n   664→            xpos = interp1d(ft_a[valid], x_r[valid], kind='linear',\n   665→                            bounds_error=False, fill_value=np.nan)(time_axis)\n   666→            ypos = interp1d(ft_a[valid], y_r[valid], kind='linear',\n   667→                            bounds_error=False, fill_value=np.nan)(time_axis)\n   668→\n   669→            if not is_tongue:\n   670→                xpos = _fill_nearest(xpos)\n   671→                ypos = _fill_nearest(ypos)\n   672→\n   673→            xvel = np.gradient(xpos)\n   674→            yvel = np.gradient(ypos)\n   675→\n   676→            if is_tongue:\n   677→                xvel[np.isnan(xvel)] = 0\n   678→                yvel[np.isnan(yvel)] = 0\n   679→            else:\n   680→                base_xvel = np.nanmedian(np.diff(xpos))\n   681→                base_yvel = np.nanmedian(np.diff(ypos))\n   682→                xvel -= (base_xvel if not np.isnan(base_xvel) else 0)\n   683→                yvel -= (base_yvel if not np.isnan(base_yvel) else 0)\n   684→                xvel = _fill_nearest(xvel)\n   685→                yvel = _fill_nearest(yvel)\n   686→\n   687→            speed[:, trix] = np.sqrt(xvel**2 + yvel**2).astype(np.float32)\n   688→        except Exception:\n   689→            continue\n   690→\n   691→    return speed\n   692→\n   693→\n   694→def _fill_nearest(arr):\n   695→    \"\"\"Fill NaN values with nearest non-NaN value.\"\"\"\n   696→    nans = np.isnan(arr)\n   697→    if not nans.any():\n   698→        return arr\n   699→    if nans.all():\n   700→        return arr\n   701→    valid_idx = np.where(~nans)[0]\n   702→    nan_idx = np.where(nans)[0]\n   703→    nearest = np.searchsorted(valid_idx, nan_idx).clip(0, len(valid_idx) - 1)\n   704→    arr[nans] = arr[valid_idx[nearest]]\n   705→    return arr\n   706→\n   707→\n   708→def _discretize_velocity(data, n_timebins, n_trials):\n   709→    \"\"\"Discretize continuous data into 2 bins using 50th percentile per session.\"\"\"\n   710→    if data is None:\n   711→        return np.zeros((n_timebins, n_trials), dtype=np.int64)\n   712→\n   713→    # Compute 50th percentile across all valid (non-NaN) values\n   714→    valid_vals = data[~np.isnan(data)]\n   715→    if valid_vals.size == 0:\n   716→        return np.zeros((n_timebins, n_trials), dtype=np.int64)\n   717→\n   718→    threshold = np.percentile(valid_vals, 50)\n   719→    disc = (data >= threshold).astype(np.int64)\n   720→    # Replace NaN entries with 0\n   721→    disc[np.isnan(data)] = 0\n   722→    return disc\n   723→\n   724→\n   725→# =============================================================================\n   726→# Plotting functions\n   727→# =============================================================================\n   728→\n   729→def plot_processing(session_data, save_path):\n   730→    \"\"\"Plot 10 ms bins\n    'smooth_window': 15,\n    'smooth_bctype': 'reflect',\n    'low_fr': 1.0,  # Hz, remove neurons below this\n    'min_units': 10,  # minimum units per session\n    'quality_exclude': {'garbage', 'noisy', 'gabrga', 'real?'},\n}\n\n\n# =============================================================================\n# HDF5 utility functions\n# =============================================================================\n\ndef h5_read_string(f, ref_or_dataset):\n    \"\"\"Read a string from HDF5, handling object references and various encodings.\"\"\"\n    if isinstance(ref_or_dataset, h5py.h5r.Reference):\n        ds = f[ref_or_dataset]\n    else:\n        ds = ref_or_dataset\n    val = ds[()]\n    if isinstance(val, bytes):\n        return val.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')\n    if isinstance(val, np.ndarray):\n        if val.dtype.kind == 'O':\n            # array of references\n            return ''.join(chr(int(f[v][()].flat[0])) for v in val.flat)\n        if val.dtype.kind in ('U', 'S'):\n            return str(val.flat[0])\n        # numeric array that represents characters (uint16)\n        if val.dtype.kind in ('u', 'i') and val.size < 200:\n            try:\n                return ''.join(chr(int(c)) for c in val.flat)\n            except (ValueError, OverflowError):\n                pass\n    if isinstance(val, (np.integer, np.floating)):\n        return str(val)\n    return str(val)\n\n\ndef h5_read_array(f, ref_or_dataset):\n    \"\"\"Read a numeric array from HDF5, handling object references.\"\"\"\n    if isinstance(ref_or_dataset, h5py.h5r.Reference):\n        ds = f[ref_or_dataset]\n    else:\n        ds = ref_or_dataset\n    val = ds[()]\n    if isinstance(val, np.ndarray):\n        return val.astype(np.float64)\n    return np.array([float(val)])\n\n\ndef h5_read_cell_array_of_arrays(f, dataset):\n    \"\"\"Read a MATLAB cell array of numeric arrays from HDF5.\"\"\"\n    refs = dataset[()]\n    result = []\n    for ref in refs.flat:\n        arr = h5_read_array(f, ref)\n        result.append(arr)\n    return result\n\n\n# =============================================================================\n# Signal processing functions (matching MATLAB reference code)\n# =============================================================================\n\ndef causal_gaussian_smooth(x, N, bctype='reflect'):\n    \"\"\"\n    Replicate mySmooth.m: causal Gaussian smoothing.\n    x: (time,) or (time, features) array\n    N: window size\n    bctype: 'reflect', 'zeropad', or 'none'\n    \"\"\"\n    if N <= 1:\n        return x\n\n    was_1d = (x.ndim == 1)\n    if was_1d:\n        x = x[:, np.newaxis]\n\n    # Build kernel\n    kern = np.array(sig_windows.gaussian(N, std=(N - 1) / (2 * 2.5)))\n    # Make causal: zero out first half\n    kern[:N // 2] = 0\n    kern = kern / kern.sum()\n\n    # Handle boundary conditions\n    if bctype == 'reflect':\n        x_filt = np.concatenate([x[:N, :], x], axis=0)\n        trim = N\n    elif bctype == 'zeropad':\n        x_filt = np.concatenate([np.zeros((N, x.shape[1])), x], axis=0)\n        trim = N\n    else:\n        x_filt = x\n        trim = 0\n\n    # Convolve each column\n    out = np.zeros_like(x_filt)\n    for j in range(x_filt.shape[1]):\n        out[:, j] = np.convolve(x_filt[:, j], kern, mode='same')\n    out = out[trim:, :]\n\n    if was_1d:\n        return out[:, 0]\n    return out\n\n\n# =============================================================================\n# Data loading functions\n# =============================================================================\n\ndef _detect_file_format(data_fn):\n    \"\"\"Detect if a .mat file is HDF5 (v7.3) or v5 format.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        f = h5py.File(data_fn, 'r')\n        f.close()\n        return 'hdf5'\n    except Exception:\n        return 'v5'\n\n\ndef _load_raw_data_v5(data_fn, probes):\n    \"\"\"Load raw data from MATLAB v5 format file. Returns common dict format.\"\"\"\n    import scipy.io as sio\n    mat = sio.loadmat(data_fn, squeeze_me=False)\n    obj = mat['obj']\n\n    # --- bp ---\n    bp_raw = obj['bp'][0, 0]\n    bp = {}\n    bp['Ntrials'] = int(bp_raw['Ntrials'][0, 0].flat[0])\n    ntrials = bp['Ntrials']\n\n    for field in ['hit', 'miss', 'no', 'early', 'L', 'R', 'autowater']:\n        bp[field] = bp_raw[field][0, 0].flatten().astype(np.float64)[:ntrials]\n\n    stim_enable = np.zeros(ntrials)\n    if 'stim' in bp_raw.dtype.names:\n        stim_raw = bp_raw['stim'][0, 0]\n        if 'enable' in stim_raw.dtype.names:\n            stim_enable = stim_raw['enable'][0, 0].flatten().astype(np.float64)[:ntrials]\n    bp['stim_enable'] = stim_enable\n\n    ev = {}\n    ev_raw = bp_raw['ev'][0, 0]\n    for field in ['goCue', 'sample', 'delay']:\n        ev[field] = ev_raw[field][0, 0].flatten().astype(np.float64)[:ntrials]\n    bp['ev'] = ev\n\n    # --- vidshift ---\n    vidshift = 0.5\n    try:\n        bit_start_arr = ev_raw['bitStart'][0, 0].flatten().astype(np.float64)\n        bit_start = np.nanmedian(bit_start_arr)\n        sglx_raw = obj['sglx'][0, 0]\n        fs = sglx_raw['fs'][0, 0].flat[0]\n        bitcode_raw = sglx_raw['bitcode'][0, 0]\n        bitcode_bitstart = np.nanmedian(bitcode_raw['bitstart'][0, 0].flatten().astype(np.float64))\n        vid_file_offset = bitcode_bitstart / fs\n        vidshift = vid_file_offset - bit_start\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n\n    # --- clu ---\n    clu_raw = obj['clu'][0, 0]\n    all_spike_times = []\n    all_spike_trials = []\n    all_qualities = []\n\n    for probe_num in probes:\n        probe_idx = probe_num - 1\n        probe_data = clu_raw[0, probe_idx]\n        n_units = probe_data.shape[1] if probe_data.ndim > 1 else probe_data.shape[0]\n        for i in range(n_units):\n            unit = probe_data[0, i] if probe_data.ndim > 1 else probe_data[i]\n            quality = str(unit['quality'].flat[0]).strip().lower() if unit['quality'].size > 0 else ''\n            trialtm = unit['trialtm'].flatten().astype(np.float64)\n            trial = unit['trial'].flatten().astype(np.float64)\n            all_qualities.append(quality)\n            all_spike_times.append(trialtm)\n            all_spike_trials.append(trial)\n\n    # --- traj (for velocity computation) ---\n    traj_data = None\n    try:\n        traj_raw = obj['traj'][0, 0]\n        traj_data = {'views': []}\n        for view_idx in range(traj_raw.shape[1]):\n            view_data = traj_raw[0, view_idx]\n            view_trials = []\n            feat_names = None\n            for t in range(min(ntrials, view_data.shape[1] if view_data.ndim > 1 else view_data.shape[0])):\n                trial_data = view_data[0, t] if view_data.ndim > 1 else view_data[t]\n                ts = trial_data['ts'].astype(np.float64)  # (nFrames, 3, nFeats) in v5\n                ft = trial_data['frameTimes'].flatten().astype(np.float64)\n                if feat_names is None:\n                    fn_raw = trial_data['featNames'].flatten()\n                    feat_names = [str(x.flat[0]).strip() for x in fn_raw]\n                view_trials.append({'ts': ts, 'frameTimes': ft, 'ts_format': 'v5'})\n            traj_data['views'].append({'trials': view_trials, 'feat_names': feat_names})\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n\n    return {\n        'bp': bp, 'vidshift': vidshift, 'ntrials': ntrials,\n        'all_spike_times': all_spike_times, 'all_spike_trials': all_spike_trials,\n        'all_qualities': all_qualities, 'traj_data': traj_data,\n        'n_units_raw': len(all_qualities),\n    }\n\n\ndef _load_raw_data_hdf5(data_fn, probes):\n    \"\"\"Load raw data from HDF5 (v7.3) format file. Returns common dict format.\"\"\"\n    f = h5py.File(data_fn, 'r')\n    obj = f['obj']\n\n    bp = {}\n    bp_group = obj['bp']\n    bp['Ntrials'] = int(bp_group['Ntrials'][()].flat[0])\n    ntrials = bp['Ntrials']\n\n    for field in ['hit', 'miss', 'no', 'early', 'L', 'R', 'autowater']:\n        bp[field] = bp_group[field][()].flatten().astype(np.float64)[:ntrials]\n\n    stim_enable = np.zeros(ntrials)\n    if 'stim' in bp_group:\n        stim_grp = bp_group['stim']\n        if 'enable' in stim_grp:\n            stim_enable = stim_grp['enable'][()].flatten().astype(np.float64)[:ntrials]\n    bp['stim_enable'] = stim_enable\n\n    ev = {}\n    ev_group = bp_group['ev']\n    for field in ['goCue', 'sample', 'delay']:\n        ev[field] = ev_group[field][()].flatten().astype(np.float64)[:ntrials]\n    bp['ev'] = ev\n\n    vidshift = 0.5\n    try:\n        bit_start = np.nanmedian(bp_group['ev']['bitStart'][()].flatten())\n        sglx = obj['sglx']\n        fs = sglx['fs'][()].flat[0]\n        bitcode_bitstart = np.nanmedian(sglx['bitcode']['bitstart'][()].flatten())\n        vid_file_offset = bitcode_bitstart / fs\n        vidshift = vid_file_offset - bit_start\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n\n    clu_refs = obj['clu'][()].flatten()\n    all_spike_times = []\n    all_spike_trials = []\n    all_qualities = []\n\n    for probe_num in probes:\n        probe_idx = probe_num - 1\n        probe_ref = clu_refs[probe_idx]\n        probe_group = f[probe_ref]\n        quality_refs = probe_group['quality'][()].flatten()\n        trialtm_refs = probe_group['trialtm'][()].flatten()\n        trial_refs = probe_group['trial'][()].flatten()\n\n        for i in range(len(quality_refs)):\n            quality = h5_read_string(f, quality_refs[i]).strip().lower()\n            trialtm = f[trialtm_refs[i]][()].flatten().astype(np.float64)\n            trial = f[trial_refs[i]][()].flatten().astype(np.float64)\n            all_qualities.append(quality)\n            all_spike_times.append(trialtm)\n            all_spike_trials.append(trial)\n\n    # --- traj ---\n    traj_data = None\n    try:\n        traj_refs = obj['traj'][()].flatten()\n        traj_data = {'views': []}\n        for view_idx in range(len(traj_refs)):\n            traj_group = f[traj_refs[view_idx]]\n            ts_refs = traj_group['ts'][()].flatten()\n            ft_refs = traj_group['frameTimes'][()].flatten()\n            feat_names_refs = traj_group['featNames'][()].flatten()\n\n            # Read feat names from first trial\n            feat_refs_trial = f[feat_names_refs[0]][()].flatten()\n            feat_names = [h5_read_string(f, fr) for fr in feat_refs_trial]\n\n            view_trials = []\n            for t in range(min(ntrials, len(ts_refs))):\n                try:\n                    ts = f[ts_refs[t]][()].astype(np.float64)  # (nFeats, 3, nFrames) in HDF5\n                    ft = f[ft_refs[t]][()].flatten().astype(np.float64)\n                    view_trials.append({'ts': ts, 'frameTimes': ft, 'ts_format': 'hdf5'})\n                except Exception:\n                    view_trials.append(None)\n            traj_data['views'].append({'trials': view_trials, 'feat_names': feat_names})\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n\n    f.close()\n\n    return {\n        'bp': bp, 'vidshift': vidshift, 'ntrials': ntrials,\n        'all_spike_times': all_spike_times, 'all_spike_trials': all_spike_trials,\n        'all_qualities': all_qualities, 'traj_data': traj_data,\n        'n_units_raw': len(all_qualities),\n    }\n\n\ndef load_session(anm, date, probes, data_dir_key, params):\n    \"\"\"\n    Load and process one session, following the reference pipeline:\n    loadObjs -> findClusters -> alignSpikes -> getSeq -> removeLowFRClusters\n    Also loads motion energy and computes kinematic features.\n    \"\"\"\n    t0 = time.time()\n    data_dir = DATA_DIRS[data_dir_key]\n    data_fn = os.path.join(data_dir, f'data_structure_{anm}_{date}.mat')\n    me_fn = os.path.join(data_dir, f'motionEnergy_{anm}_{date}.mat')\n\n    session_id = f'{anm}_{date}'\n\n    if not os.path.exists(data_fn):\n        print(f\"  WARNING: {data_fn} not found, skipping\")\n        return None\n\n    # Detect format and load raw data\n    fmt = _detect_file_format(data_fn)\n    try:\n        if fmt == 'hdf5':\n            raw = _load_raw_data_hdf5(data_fn, probes)\n        else:\n            raw = _load_raw_data_v5(data_fn, probes)\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"  WARNING: Could not load {session_id}: {e}\")\n        return None\n\n    bp = raw['bp']\n    ntrials = raw['ntrials']\n    vidshift = raw['vidshift']\n    all_spike_times = raw['all_spike_times']\n    all_spike_trials = raw['all_spike_trials']\n    all_qualities = raw['all_qualities']\n    traj_data = raw['traj_data']\n    n_units_before_quality = raw['n_units_raw']\n    ev = bp['ev']\n\n    # --- Filter clusters by quality ---\n    quality_exclude = params['quality_exclude']\n    keep_mask = np.array([q not in quality_exclude and q != '' and q != 'nan'\n                          for q in all_qualities])\n    good_indices = np.where(keep_mask)[0]\n\n    if len(good_indices) == 0:\n        print(f\"  WARNING: {session_id} has no units after quality filter, skipping\")\n        return None\n\n    # --- Trial filtering ---\n    trial_mask = (bp['early'] == 0) & (bp['stim_enable'] == 0)\n    valid_trials = np.where(trial_mask)[0] + 1  # 1-indexed\n    if len(valid_trials) < 2:\n        print(f\"  WARNING: {session_id} has < 2 valid trials, skipping\")\n        return None\n\n    # --- Align spikes to goCue and bin ---\n    align_times = ev[params['align_event']]\n    edges = np.arange(params['tmin'], params['tmax'] + params['dt'], params['dt'])\n    time_axis = edges[:-1] + params['dt'] / 2\n    n_timebins = len(time_axis)\n    n_valid_trials = len(valid_trials)\n\n    trialdat = np.zeros((len(good_indices), n_timebins, n_valid_trials), dtype=np.float32)\n\n    for neuron_idx, clu_idx in enumerate(good_indices):\n        spike_tm = all_spike_times[clu_idx]\n        spike_trial = all_spike_trials[clu_idx]\n\n        for t_idx, trial_num in enumerate(valid_trials):\n            spk_mask = spike_trial == trial_num\n            if not np.any(spk_mask):\n                continue\n            spk_times = spike_tm[spk_mask] - align_times[trial_num - 1]\n            counts, _ = np.histogram(spk_times, bins=edges)\n            fr = causal_gaussian_smooth(counts.astype(np.float64) / params['dt'],\n                                       params['smooth_window'],\n                                       params['smooth_bctype'])\n            trialdat[neuron_idx, :, t_idx] = fr.astype(np.float32)\n\n    # --- Remove low FR clusters ---\n    mean_fr = np.mean(trialdat, axis=(1, 2))\n    fr_mask = mean_fr > params['low_fr']\n    trialdat = trialdat[fr_mask, :, :]\n    n_neurons = trialdat.shape[0]\n\n    if n_neurons < params['min_units']:\n        print(f\"  WARNING: {session_id} has {n_neurons} units (< {params['min_units']}), skipping\")\n        return None\n\n    # --- Extract trial-level variables ---\n    valid_trial_indices = valid_trials - 1\n    lick_direction = bp['R'][valid_trial_indices].copy()\n    behavioral_context = 1.0 - bp['autowater'][valid_trial_indices]\n    outcome = bp['hit'][valid_trial_indices].copy()\n\n    # --- Load Motion Energy ---\n    me_data = None\n    if os.path.exists(me_fn):\n        try:\n            me_data = _load_motion_energy_generic(me_fn, traj_data, vidshift,\n                                                  align_times, time_axis, ntrials)\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"  WARNING: Could not load motion energy for {session_id}: {e}\")\n\n    # --- Compute tongue and paw velocity from DLC ---\n    tongue_vel = _compute_feature_velocity_generic(\n        traj_data, ntrials, view=2, feat_name='top_tongue',\n        vidshift=vidshift, align_times=align_times,\n        time_axis=time_axis, is_tongue=True)\n\n    paw_vel = _compute_feature_velocity_generic(\n        traj_data, ntrials, view=2, feat_name='top_paw',\n        vidshift=vidshift, align_times=align_times,\n        time_axis=time_axis, is_tongue=False)\n\n    # --- Subsample to valid trials ---\n    valid_0idx = valid_trial_indices  # 0-indexed\n\n    if tongue_vel is not None:\n        tongue_vel = tongue_vel[:, valid_0idx]\n    if paw_vel is not None:\n        paw_vel = paw_vel[:, valid_0idx]\n    if me_data is not None:\n        me_data = me_data[:, valid_0idx]\n\n    # --- Discretize continuous outputs (50th percentile per session) ---\n    tongue_vel_disc = _discretize_velocity(tongue_vel, n_timebins, n_valid_trials)\n    paw_vel_disc = _discretize_velocity(paw_vel, n_timebins, n_valid_trials)\n    me_disc = _discretize_velocity(me_data, n_timebins, n_valid_trials)\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"  {session_id}: {n_neurons} neurons, {n_valid_trials} trials, \"\n          f\"{n_units_before_quality} raw units -> {int(keep_mask.sum())} after quality -> \"\n          f\"{n_neurons} after FR filter ({elapsed:.1f}s)\")\n\n    return {\n        'session_id': session_id,\n        'animal': anm,\n        'date': date,\n        'n_neurons': n_neurons,\n        'n_trials': n_valid_trials,\n        'time_axis': time_axis,\n        'trialdat': trialdat,  # (n_neurons, n_timebins, n_trials)\n        'lick_direction': lick_direction,\n        'behavioral_context': behavioral_context,\n        'outcome': outcome,\n        'tongue_vel_disc': tongue_vel_disc,  # (n_timebins, n_trials)\n        'paw_vel_disc': paw_vel_disc,\n        'me_disc': me_disc,\n        'tongue_vel_raw': tongue_vel,\n        'paw_vel_raw': paw_vel,\n        'me_raw': me_data,\n    }\n\n\ndef _load_motion_energy_generic(me_fn, traj_data, vidshift, align_times, time_axis, ntrials):\n    \"\"\"Load motion energy and align to neural time axis following loadMotionEnergy.m\"\"\"\n    import scipy.io as sio\n\n    me_cell = None\n    try:\n        me_file = sio.loadmat(me_fn, squeeze_me=True)\n        me_struct = me_file['me']\n        me_cell = me_struct['data'].item()\n    except Exception:\n        me_h5 = h5py.File(me_fn, 'r')\n        me_grp = me_h5['me']\n        me_data_refs = me_grp['data'][()].flatten()\n        me_cell = [me_h5[ref][()].flatten().astype(np.float64) for ref in me_data_refs]\n        me_h5.close()\n\n    side_cam = traj_data['views'][0] if traj_data is not None else None\n    n_time = len(time_axis)\n    me_aligned = np.full((n_time, ntrials), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n\n    for trix in range(ntrials):\n        try:\n            me_trial = me_cell[trix].flatten().astype(np.float64) if hasattr(me_cell[trix], 'flatten') else np.array([float(me_cell[trix])])\n        except (IndexError, TypeError):\n            continue\n        if me_trial.size == 0:\n            continue\n\n        ft = None\n        if side_cam is not None and trix < len(side_cam['trials']) and side_cam['trials'][trix] is not None:\n            ft = side_cam['trials'][trix]['frameTimes']\n        if ft is None or ft.size == 0 or np.all(np.isnan(ft)):\n            ft = np.arange(1, me_trial.size + 1) / 400.0\n\n        ft_aligned = ft - vidshift - align_times[trix]\n        min_len = min(len(ft_aligned), len(me_trial))\n        ft_aligned, me_trial = ft_aligned[:min_len], me_trial[:min_len]\n        valid = ~np.isnan(ft_aligned) & ~np.isnan(me_trial)\n        if valid.sum() < 2:\n            continue\n        try:\n            me_aligned[:, trix] = interp1d(ft_aligned[valid], me_trial[valid],\n                                           kind='linear', bounds_error=False,\n                                           fill_value=np.nan)(time_axis).astype(np.float32)\n        except Exception:\n            continue\n\n    for trix me_aligned\n\n\ndef _compute_feature_velocity_generic(traj_data, ntrials, view, feat_name, vidshift, align_times, time_axis, is_tongue=False):\n    \"\"\"Compute velocity of a DLC feature from traj_data (format-agnostic).\"\"\"\n    n_time = len(time_axis)\n    speed = np.full((n_time, ntrials), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n\n    if traj_data is None:\n        return None\n\n    view_idx = _fill_nearest(ypos)\n\n            xvel = np.gradient(xpos)\n            yvel = np.gradient(ypos)\n\n            if is_tongue:\n                xvel[np.isnan(xvel)] = 0\n                yvel[np.isnan(yvel)] = 0\n            else:\n                base_xvel = np.nanmedian(np.diff(xpos))\n                base_yvel = np.nanmedian(np.diff(ypos))\n                xvel -= (base_xvel if not np.isnan(base_xvel) else 0)\n                yvel -= (base_yvel if not np.isnan(base_yvel) else 0)\n                xvel = _fill_nearest(xvel)\n                yvel = _fill_nearest(yvel)\n\n            speed[:, trix] = np.sqrt(xvel**2 + yvel**2).astype(np.float32)\n        except Exception:\n            continue\n\n    return speed\n\n\ndef _fill_nearest(arr):\n    \"\"\"Fill NaN values with nearest non-NaN value.\"\"\"\n    nans = np.isnan(arr)\n    if not nans.any():\n        return arr\n    if nans.all():\n        return arr\n    valid_idx = np.where(~nans)[0]\n    nan_idx = np.where(nans)[0]\n    nearest = np.searchsorted(valid_idx, nan_idx).clip(0, len(valid_idx) - 1)\n    arr[nans] = arr[valid_idx[nearest]]\n    return arr\n\n\ndef _discretize_velocity(data, n_timebins, n_trials):\n    \"\"\"Discretize continuous data into 2 bins using 50th percentile per session.\"\"\"\n    if data is None:\n        return np.zeros((n_timebins, n_trials), dtype=np.int64)\n\n    # Compute 50th percentile across all valid (non-NaN) values\n    valid_vals = data[~np.isnan(data)]\n    if valid_vals.size == 0:\n        return np.zeros((n_timebins, n_trials), dtype=np.int64)\n\n    threshold = np.percentile(valid_vals, 50)\n    disc = (data >= threshold).astype(np.int64)\n    # Replace NaN entries with 0\n    disc[np.isnan(data)] = 0\n    return tmin:dt:tmax\n   131→- **Smoothing**: Causal Gaussian kernel, window=15 (via mySmooth)\n   132→- **Firing rates**: spks/sec (spike count / dt)\n   133→- **Video offset**: subtract 0.5s from frameTimes Quality filter: Exclude 'garbage', 'noisy', 'gabrga', 'real?' (quality='all' mode)\n   141→2. Low firing rate: Remove neurons with mean FR < 1 Hz across all trials\n   142→3. Min units: Sessions with < 10 units excluded\n   143→\n   144→**Trial curation rules**:\n   145→1. Exclude early lick trials (~early)\n   146→2. Exclude stim/photoinactivation trials (~stim.enable)\n   147→3. For hit analyses: use hit trials only\n   148→4. For DR: ~autowater (autowater=0)\n   149→5. CONVERSION_NOTES.md documents: \"Exclude early lick and stim/photoinactivation trials (~early, ~stim.enable)\". No mention of filtering trials past recording end.\n\n## 2-a. What variables in the raw data is the `neural` data derived from?\n\ni. The spike-sorted clusters from `obj.clu`, specifically each cluster's `trialtm` (spike times relative to trial start), `trial` (trial assignment per spike), and `quality` (curation label). Counts are divided by `dt` to get firing rates in Hz. The rates are smoothed with a **causal** Gaussian kernel of window size 15, implemented via `causal_gaussian_smooth` which replicates `mySmooth.m`. The causal kernel zeros out the first half of the Gaussian window.\n\nii.\n```python\nPARAMS = {\n    'dt': 1.0 / 100,  # 10 ms bins\n    'smooth_window': 15,\n    ...\n}\n\nedges = np.arange(params['tmin'], params['tmax'] + params['dt'], params['dt'])\ncounts, _ = np.histogram(spk_times, bins=edges)\nfr = causal_gaussian_smooth(counts.astype(np.float64) / params['dt'],\n                           params['smooth_window'],\n                           params['smooth_bctype'])\n```\n\nCausal smoothing:\n```python\ndef causal_gaussian_smooth(x, N, bctype='reflect'):\n    kern = np.array(sig_windows.gaussian(N, std=(N - 1) / (2 * 2.5)))\n    kern[:N // 2] = 0  # Make causal\n    kern = kern / kern.sum()\n    ...\n```\n\niii. CONVERSION_NOTES.md: \"dt=1/100 (10ms bins)\", \"Causal Gaussian kernel, window=15\". How is the `neural` data filtered based on quality controls?\n\ni. Three filters. (1) Cluster quality: labels matching `{'garbage', 'noisy', 'gabrga', 'real?'}` are excluded, as well as empty or 'nan' labels. (2) Firing rate: units with mean FR <= 1 Hz are removed. Counts are divided by `dt` to get firing rates in Hz. The causal kernel zeros out the first half of the Gaussian window.\n\nii.\n```python\nPARAMS = {\n    'dt': 1.0 / 100,  # 10 ms bins\n    'smooth_window': 15,\n    ...\n}\n\nedges = np.arange(params['tmin'], params['tmax'] + params['dt'], params['dt'])\ncounts, _ = np.histogram(spk_times, bins=edges)\nfr = causal_gaussian_smooth(counts.astype(np.float64) / params['dt'],\n                           params['smooth_window'],\n                           params['smooth_bctype'])\n```\n\nCausal smoothing:\n```python\ndef causal_gaussian_smooth(x, N, bctype='reflect'):\n    kern = np.array(sig_windows.gaussian(N, std=(N - 1) / (2 * 2.5)))\n    kern[:N // 2] = 0  # Make causal\n    kern = kern / kern.sum()\n    ...\n```\n\niii. CONVERSION_NOTES.md: \"dt=1/100 (10ms bins)\", \"Causal Gaussian kernel, window=15\". The AI chose 10ms bins based on `WorkingWithDataObjs.m` tutorial parameter `dt=1/100`, and causal smoothing based on `mySmooth.m`.\n\n## 2-c.
